📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期SARS-CoV-2研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 疫苗 整体有效性为 75%(95% CI 70%–79%),既往于 2023-2024 年接种过者有效性为 84%(95% CI 80%–87%),而未接种者为 65%(95% CI 34%–82%)。
  • 在自然感染个体中,N 的 IgG 阳性率 (80.9%) 高于 S (42.8%),与大流行前对照相比,N 特异性 IgG 水平显着升高(p 值 <0.05)。接种疫苗的 队列研究 对两种低 IgM 水平的抗原表现出高 IgG 阳性率 (83-100%),并且总体 IgG 和亚类反应相对于大流行前对照显着升高(p 值 <0.05)。
  • CC65.1 的工程变体显示出新获得的 MERS-CoV 中和活性,高分辨率结构研究表明关键的 突变 重塑了互补位,以更好地适应和稳定 MERS-CoV S2 茎螺旋,从而提高结合亲和力和中和效力。
  • 三年半时,持续症状很常见:疲劳(65.6%)、呼吸困难(62.5%)和注意力不集中(65.6%)。血管内皮生长因子 D 和血管生成素-1 仍然升高。部分患者的 α-2-巨球蛋白保持较高水平,凝血酶原时间延长。
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The total sample size was 176169, consisting of 169117 males and 7,726 females. In most studies, the average age was reported to be around 40 years. [10.1017/dmp.2026.10392].
  • The available observational evidence did not show a clear increase in mortality among transferred compared with non-transferred COVID-19 ICU patients.
  • The vaccine effectiveness was 75% (95% CI 70%–79%) overall, with higher effectiveness in individuals previously vaccinated in 2023–2024 (84%, 95% CI 80%–87%) compared to those without prior vaccination (65%, 95% CI 34%–82%).
  • Among naturally infected individuals, the IgG-positivity rate was higher for N (80.9%) than S (42.8%), with significantly elevated N-specific IgG levels compared to prepandemic controls (p-value <0.05).

📘 主报告：研究论文 / Primary Research

Journal: Microbiology Spectrum  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-05  |  DOI: 10.1128/spectrum.04127-25  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, e0412725
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

CDR-H3 突变 的变构效应调节保守的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 RBD 靶向抗体的结合和中和

Allosteric effects of CDR-H3 mutations modulate binding and neutralization of a conserved SARS-CoV-2 RBD-targeting antibody
作者： Muhammad Waqas Nasir, Qiyun Liang, Jun He, Umer Rashid, Jia-Bing Wu, Yong Gao
摘要中文翻译未标记:进化 抗原 进化 继续削弱第一代单克隆抗体的活性,强调了识别刺突受体结合域 (RBD) 内保守特征的抗体的价值。作为面向广度的工程模型,我们评估了 RBD 导向的抗体 XG83,它具有 CDRH3 主导的互补位。利用 XG83-Wuhan RBD 晶体结构作为参考,我们将 MOE 丙氨酸扫描和残基扫描与 CDRH3 (A116-Y132) 集成,以识别化学上合理的替代物并测试与 Omicron BA.1 RBD 的相互作用。比较对接中以亲本 CDRH3 为中心的姿势比突变体 MuXG83 (-254.2) 具有更好的分数 (-280.18),并且 100 ns 分子动力学表明 MuXG83 稳定性较差(具有较高的 RMSD/RMSF 和较差的相互作用能)。针对 Omicron BA.1 RBD 的 ELISA 结果表明,XG83 的结合力比 MuXG83 强 40%;收敛仅发生在最高浓度,这与 SPR 研究以及 突变 增加解离的事实一致。根据这种结合间隙,BA.1 假病毒中和表明 XG83 比 MuXG83 更强大,表明 E118L/F130H CDR-H3 改变降低了针对 Omicron BA.1 的功能活性。这些结果意味着非表位 CDR-H3 残基对界面稳定性和构象动力学的变构影响会影响抗体性能。功能测试仅限于 Omicron BA.1;因此,需要更大规模的变异组研究来确定此类 突变 是否会影响抗体广度。我们的研究结果强调了优化互补位的结构引导方法,并表明 CDRH3 的非表位(潜在变构)变化在研究进展前的潜在发展和中和作用时也很重要。重要性:这项研究展示了 CDR-H3 中适度的变构特征如何控制中和效力和广度之间的微妙平衡,为 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 抗体工程做出了及时而重要的补充。本研究利用晶体学、丙氨酸扫描、残基扫描、对接、分子动力学以及实验 ELISA 和中和测定,为合理互补位优化提供了一个结构引导框架。调节长程稳定性而不仅仅是直接表位接触的 CDR-H3 残基的发现揭示了抗体设计中未被重视的方面,并解释了为什么一些改变改善了预期的相互作用但降低了功能性能。重要的是,野生型 XG83 和修饰后的 MuXG83 的比较显示了变构调节如何影响 Omicron 等变异体中抗体-抗原的相容性。这些发现阐明了保守的 RBD 识别,并为构建对病毒 进化 更具抵抗力的下一代治疗抗体提供了策略。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 抗原性 进化 已降低首代单克隆抗体的有效性,亟需针对刺突蛋白受体结合域(RBD)内保守区域的抗体。本研究旨在通过评估高度依赖其 CDR-H3 抗原结合片段(paratope)进行结合的 RBD 导向抗体 XG83,对面向广谱性的抗体工程进行建模。
设计与对象
该研究使用结构引导框架,结合晶体学、丙氨酸和残基扫描、对接、分子动力学和实验测定,将野生型抗体 XG83 与修饰变体 MuXG83 进行比较,重点关注 CDRH3 与 Omicron BA.1 RBD 的相互作用。
核心方法
晶体学提供了 XG83-Wuhan RBD 参考结构,而基于 MOE 的丙氨酸和残基扫描确定了 CDRH3 (A116-Y132) 替代品。对接和 100 纳秒分子动力学评估稳定性和相互作用能,ELISA、表面等离子共振 (SPR) 和假病毒中和测量针对 Omicron BA.1 的结合和功能活性。
主要结果
亲本 XG83 表现出更好的对接分数(-280.18 vs. -254.2)和更高的分子动力学稳定性,比 MuXG83 具有更低的 RMSD/RMSF 和更有利的相互作用能。ELISA 和 SPR 显示 XG83 的结合力比 MuXG83 强 40%,假病毒中和表明 XG83 对抗 Omicron BA.1 的效力明显更强。
解释与创新
作者解释说,非表位 CDRH3 残基对界面稳定性和构象动力学发挥变构作用,从而调节抗体性能。他们强调,这种变构调整是抗体设计中一个未被充分认识的方面,揭示了尽管改善了预测的相互作用,但细微的变化如何会意外地降低功能活性。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现证明 CDRH3 的变构调节可以增强对病毒 进化 的抵抗力,为下一代治疗抗体的工程设计提供了信息。这种方法可以指导针对新兴 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体具有更广泛中和能力的抗体的开发。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于针对 Omicron BA.1 的功能测试,因此对其他变体的影响广度存在不确定性。证据是温和的,因为结论是基于单个变体,并依赖于通过实验分析验证的计算预测,需要在更广泛的变体小组中进一步验证。

Allosteric effects of CDR-H3 mutations modulate binding and neutralization of a conserved SARS-CoV-2 RBD-targeting antibody

Authors: Muhammad Waqas Nasir, Qiyun Liang, Jun He, Umer Rashid, Jia-Bing Wu, Yong Gao
Original abstract (English)
UNLABELLED: SARS-CoV-2 antigenic evolution continues to erode the activity of first-generation monoclonal antibodies, underscoring the value of antibodies that recognize conserved features within the spike receptor-binding domain (RBD). As a model for breadth-oriented engineering, we assessed the RBD-directed antibody XG83, which has a CDRH3-dominated paratope. Utilizing the XG83-Wuhan RBD crystal structure as a reference, we integrated MOE alanine scanning and residue scanning with CDRH3 (A116-Y132) to identify chemically reasonable replacements and test interaction to an Omicron BA.1 RBD. The parental CDRH3-centric pose in comparative docking had a better score (-280.18) than the mutant MuXG83 (-254.2), along with 100-ns molecular dynamics showed that MuXG83 was less stable (with higher RMSD/RMSF with less favorable interaction energy). The ELISA results against Omicron BA.1 RBD demonstrated that XG83 had 40% stronger binding than MuXG83; convergence happened only at the highest concentration, concordant with SPR studies and the fact that mutation increased dissociation. According to this binding gap, BA.1 pseudovirus neutralization demonstrated that XG83 was much more powerful than MuXG83, showing that the E118L/F130H CDR-H3 alterations decreased functional activity against Omicron BA.1. These results imply that allosteric influences on interface stability and conformational dynamics by non-epitope CDR-H3 residues can affect antibody performance. Functional testing was confined to Omicron BA.1; therefore, larger variant-panel studies are needed to ascertain if such mutations affect antibody breadth. Our findings highlight a structure-guided approach for optimizing paratopes and indicate that non-epitope (potentially allosteric) changes to CDRH3 are also important while investigating potential development and neutralization before advancement. IMPORTANCE: This study shows how modest allosteric characteristics in CDR-H3 control the delicate balance between neutralizing potency and breadth, making a timely and significant addition to SARS-CoV-2 antibody engineering. Using crystallography, alanine scanning, residue scanning, docking, molecular dynamics, and experimental ELISA and neutralization assays, this study offers a structure-guided framework for rational paratope optimization. The discovery of CDR-H3 residues that regulate long-range stability rather than just direct epitope contacts reveals an unappreciated aspect of antibody design and explains why some alterations improve anticipated interactions but degrade functional performance. Importantly, the comparison of wild-type XG83 and modified MuXG83 shows how allosteric tuning affects antibody-antigen compatibility in developing variations like Omicron. These findings illuminate conserved RBD recognition and offer strategies for building next-generation therapeutic antibodies that are more resistant to viral evolution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
SARS-CoV-2 antigenic evolution has reduced the efficacy of first-generation monoclonal antibodies, creating a need for antibodies that target conserved regions within the spike receptor-binding domain (RBD). The study aimed to model breadth-oriented antibody engineering by assessing the RBD-directed antibody XG83, which relies heavily on its CDRH3 paratope for binding.
Design and population
The study used a structure-guided framework combining crystallography, alanine and residue scanning, docking, molecular dynamics, and experimental assays to compare the wild-type antibody XG83 with a modified variant, MuXG83, focusing on CDRH3 interactions with the Omicron BA.1 RBD.
Core methods
Crystallography provided the XG83-Wuhan RBD reference structure, while MOE-based alanine and residue scanning identified CDRH3 (A116-Y132) replacements. Docking and 100-ns molecular dynamics assessed stability and interaction energy, and ELISA, surface plasmon resonance (SPR), and pseudovirus neutralization measured binding and functional activity against Omicron BA.1.
Main results
The parental XG83 showed a better docking score (-280.18 vs. -254.2) and greater stability in molecular dynamics, with lower RMSD/RMSF and more favorable interaction energy than MuXG83. ELISA and SPR revealed that XG83 had 40% stronger binding than MuXG83, and pseudovirus neutralization demonstrated that XG83 was significantly more potent against Omicron BA.1.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that non-epitope CDRH3 residues exert allosteric effects on interface stability and conformational dynamics, thereby modulating antibody performance. They highlight that such allosteric tuning is an underappreciated aspect of antibody design, revealing how subtle changes can unexpectedly degrade functional activity despite improving predicted interactions.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings inform the engineering of next-generation therapeutic antibodies by demonstrating that allosteric modulation of CDRH3 can enhance resistance to viral evolution. This approach may guide the development of antibodies with broader neutralization capacity against emerging SARS-CoV-2 variants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to functional testing against Omicron BA.1, leaving uncertainty about the breadth of effects across other variants. The evidence is moderate, as the conclusions are based on a single variant and rely on computational predictions validated by experimental assays, warranting further validation in broader variant panels.

疫苗接种 在预防 2019 年严重冠状病毒病中的关键作用:澳大利亚肾移植受者 队列研究 临床结果的观点

Critical role of vaccination in preventing severe coronavirus disease 2019: perspectives from clinical outcomes in an Australian kidney transplant recipient cohort
作者： Dhakshayini Tharmaraj, Paayal Naidu, Mohana Baptista, Sarah McBride, Rhonda L. Stuart, Claire Dendle, Kevan R. Polkinghorne, William R. Mulley
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Kidney transplant recipients (KTRs) face disproportionately severe outcomes from COVID-19, and vaccine hesitancy due to concerns about allograft rejection may further increase risk. This study aimed to quantify COVID-19 vaccine uptake, infection outcomes, and rejection risk in KTRs during the Delta and Omicron waves.
设计与对象
队列研究 研究在单一中心进行,招募了截至 2021 年 3 月 22 日具有功能性同种异体移植物的所有成年 KTR。研究期间涵盖 Delta 和 Omicron (BA.1/BA.2) 波。
核心方法
Vaccination status and KTR-related risk factors were assessed using Cox proportional hazards models to determine predictors of severe COVID-19 infection (hospitalisation), death, and all infections. Rejection risk following infection or vaccination was also evaluated.
主要结果
Among 986 KTRs, 333 (33.8%) had COVID-19 infections, 79 (23.7%) were severe, and 13 deaths occurred (3.9%). Each additional vaccine dose reduced the risk of severe infection (adjusted hazards ratio 0.5; 95% CI 0.40–0.65; P < 0.001) and death. Older age, lower eGFR, diabetes, and prior allograft rejection predicted severe infection, while male gender predicted all infections. Vaccine type and immunosuppressant doses did not predict outcomes, and neither infections nor vaccinations increased rejection risk.
解释与创新
The study demonstrates that higher vaccine uptake significantly reduces severe COVID-19 and mortality in KTRs without increasing rejection risk, addressing concerns about vaccine-induced allograft damage. This provides direct clinical evidence supporting optimal vaccination in this high-risk group.
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在 KTR 中优先考虑 疫苗接种 以预防严重疾病和 死亡率,同时让临床医生和患者放心排斥风险较低。对具有复合危险因素的免疫功能低下患者进行有针对性的干预是必要的。
局限与证据强度
单中心设计和缺乏随机性限制了普遍性和因果推断。然而,强有力的统计调整和明确的结果定义强化了证据。该研究为 疫苗 在 KTR 中的功效提供了中等强度的证据。

Critical role of vaccination in preventing severe coronavirus disease 2019: perspectives from clinical outcomes in an Australian kidney transplant recipient cohort

Authors: Dhakshayini Tharmaraj, Paayal Naidu, Mohana Baptista, Sarah McBride, Rhonda L. Stuart, Claire Dendle, Kevan R. Polkinghorne, William R. Mulley
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Kidney transplant recipients (KTRs) disproportionately experience severe COVID-19 infections. We previously identified concern regarding vaccine-induced rejection as a barrier to vaccine uptake. AIM: This study assesses COVID-19 vaccine uptake, infection outcomes and allograft rejection in KTRs during the delta and omicron waves (BA.1/BA.2). METHODS: This cohort study included all adult KTRs with a functioning allograft at 22 March 2021 at our centre. KTR and vaccination-related risk factors for the primary outcome of severe COVID-19 infection (requiring hospitalisation) and the secondary outcomes of death and all COVID-19 infections were assessed using Cox proportional hazards models. Rejection risk following infection/vaccination was also assessed. RESULTS: Of the 986 included KTRs, there were 333 (33.8%) COVID-19 infections, 79 (23.7%) severe infections and 13 deaths (n = 13/333, 3.9%). Vaccine number was the most significant modifiable predictor of severe infection (adjusted hazards ratio, per additional dose, 0.5; 95% confidence interval, 0.40-0.65, P < 0.001) and death. While older age also predicted severe infection and death, lower estimated glomerular filtration rate, history of diabetes and previous allograft rejection predicted severe infection. Male gender was the only predictor of all infections. Mycophenolate dose, prednisolone use and vaccine type did not predict infection or severe disease. Infections and vaccinations did not increase the risk of rejection. CONCLUSIONS: KTRs should be encouraged to be optimally vaccinated to prevent severe disease and can be reassured about the low risk of allograft rejection. Risk factors that compound the state of immunocompromise and severe COVID-19 infections should prompt vigilance and targeted interventions to mitigate these risks.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Kidney transplant recipients (KTRs) face disproportionately severe outcomes from COVID-19, and vaccine hesitancy due to concerns about allograft rejection may further increase risk. This study aimed to quantify COVID-19 vaccine uptake, infection outcomes, and rejection risk in KTRs during the Delta and Omicron waves.
Design and population
A cohort study was conducted at a single centre, enrolling all adult KTRs with a functioning allograft as of 22 March 2021. The study period covered the Delta and Omicron (BA.1/BA.2) waves.
Core methods
Vaccination status and KTR-related risk factors were assessed using Cox proportional hazards models to determine predictors of severe COVID-19 infection (hospitalisation), death, and all infections. Rejection risk following infection or vaccination was also evaluated.
Main results
Among 986 KTRs, 333 (33.8%) had COVID-19 infections, 79 (23.7%) were severe, and 13 deaths occurred (3.9%). Each additional vaccine dose reduced the risk of severe infection (adjusted hazards ratio 0.5; 95% CI 0.40–0.65; P < 0.001) and death. Older age, lower eGFR, diabetes, and prior allograft rejection predicted severe infection, while male gender predicted all infections. Vaccine type and immunosuppressant doses did not predict outcomes, and neither infections nor vaccinations increased rejection risk.
Interpretation and novelty
The study demonstrates that higher vaccine uptake significantly reduces severe COVID-19 and mortality in KTRs without increasing rejection risk, addressing concerns about vaccine-induced allograft damage. This provides direct clinical evidence supporting optimal vaccination in this high-risk group.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support prioritising vaccination in KTRs to prevent severe disease and mortality, while reassuring clinicians and patients about the low risk of rejection. Targeted interventions for immunocompromised patients with compounding risk factors are warranted.
Limitations and evidence strength
The single-centre design and lack of randomisation limit generalisability and causal inference. However, robust statistical adjustment and clear outcome definitions strengthen the evidence. The study provides moderate-strength evidence for vaccine efficacy in KTRs.

Journal: Biomedicine & Pharmacotherapy  |  Canonical publication date: 2026-08-04  |  DOI: 10.1016/j.biopha.2026.119831  |  Date basis: first_publication_date  |  Publication status: Current window  |  Citation: 2026, 202, 119831
A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

静脉注射免疫球蛋白重新校准单核细胞对跨病毒变体的流产 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的反应

Intravenous immunoglobulin recalibrates monocyte responses to abortive SARS-CoV-2 infection across viral variants
作者： Meryem Alaka, Benoit Desnues
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Monocytes contribute to inflammation during coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), yet their capacity to support productive infection by severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), and the impact of viral evolution on their responses, remain unclear.
设计与对象
在这里,我们研究了原代人类单核细胞和代表不同进化阶段的三种 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 变体(Wuhan、Delta 和 Omicron)之间的相互作用,并评估了静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIg) 的调节作用。
核心方法
所有变体均有效进入单核细胞并作为细胞内病毒RNA和蛋白质持续存在,但未能产生感染性后代,表明感染失败。
主要结果
与武汉和三角洲相比,Omicron 感染与复制相关特征增强有关。
解释与创新
IVIg 不能阻止病毒进入或 RNA 持续存在,但显着减少感染诱导的空泡化并选择性调节细胞因子反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些效应的发生与中和活性无关。
局限与证据强度
总之,这些发现将原代人类单核细胞确定为流产但免疫活性的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的靶标,并证明 IVIg 通过 Fc 依赖性机制而不是直接病毒中和来调节单核细胞应激和炎症反应。

Intravenous immunoglobulin recalibrates monocyte responses to abortive SARS-CoV-2 infection across viral variants

Authors: Meryem Alaka, Benoit Desnues
Original abstract (English)
Monocytes contribute to inflammation during coronavirus disease 2019 (COVID-19), yet their capacity to support productive infection by severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2), and the impact of viral evolution on their responses, remain unclear. Here, we investigated the interaction between primary human monocytes and three SARS-CoV-2 variants representing distinct evolutionary stages (Wuhan, Delta, and Omicron), and evaluated the modulatory effects of intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIg). All variants efficiently entered monocytes and persisted as intracellular viral RNA and protein, but failed to generate infectious progeny, indicating abortive infection. Despite the absence of productive replication, infected monocytes exhibited marked ultrastructural remodeling and mounted robust innate immune responses. Omicron infection was associated with enhanced replication-related signatures compared with Wuhan and Delta. IVIg did not prevent viral entry or RNA persistence but significantly reduced infection-induced vacuolization and selectively modulated cytokine responses. Notably, high-dose IVIg enhanced TNF-associated responses and reduced IL10 expression, suggesting a recalibration of monocyte inflammatory set-points. These effects occurred independently of neutralizing activity. Together, these findings identify primary human monocytes as targets of abortive yet immunologically active SARS-CoV-2 infection and demonstrate that IVIg modulate monocyte stress and inflammatory responses through Fc-dependent mechanisms rather than direct viral neutralization.

mRNA-1273 和 BNT162b2 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 二价加强剂 疫苗 在美国疗养院居民中的免疫原性比较。

Comparative immunogenicity of mRNA-1273 and BNT162b2 SARS-CoV-2 bivalent booster vaccines in US nursing home residents.
作者： Olajide J. Olagunju, Oladayo A. Oyebanji, Debbie Keresztesy, Dennis Wilk, Evan Dickerson, Tiffany Wallace, Laurel Holland, Michael C Payne, Ellen See, Chia Jung Li
摘要中文翻译疗养院居民 (NHR) 仍然是最容易遭受 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染严重后果的人群之一。虽然 mRNA-1273(Spikevax,Moderna)和 BNT162b2(Comirnaty,Pfizer-BioNTech)疫苗 广泛用于该人群,但其免疫原性的比较数据,特别是在二价加强剂后,仍然有限。我们对接受 mRNA-1273 或 BNT162b2 二价 疫苗 的美国 NHR 进行了纵向免疫学评估。疫苗接种 后 10-30 天收集血清样本。使用基于 Luminex 珠的测定法测量抗尖峰 IgG 水平,并通过假病毒中和评估中和滴度。使用双边 Wilcoxon 秩和检验进行效价分布的比较分析。两个 疫苗 组均表现出对祖先武汉株和 Omicron BA.4/5 亚变体的强烈体液反应。mRNA -1273 和 BNT162b2 疫苗 制剂之间的刺突结合抗体水平和中和滴度相当。结论:mRNA-1273 和 BNT162b2 mRNA 疫苗 在 NHR 中都能引发类似的强大体液免疫,支持它们在加强策略中的互换使用。我们的研究结果强调了及时加强注射比精确产品选择对于保护这一高风险、免疫衰老人群的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
疗养院居民仍然极易遭受 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的严重后果,但该人群中 mRNA-1273 和 BNT162b2 二价加强剂 疫苗 的免疫原性比较数据有限。
设计与对象
在接受 mRNA-1273 或 BNT162b2 二价 疫苗 的美国疗养院居民中进行了纵向免疫学评估。
核心方法
疫苗接种 后 10-30 天收集血清样本,并使用基于 Luminex 珠的测定法测量抗尖峰 IgG 水平,同时通过假病毒中和评估中和滴度。使用双边 Wilcoxon 秩和检验进行效价分布的比较分析。
主要结果
疫苗 组均表现出对祖先武汉毒株和 Omicron BA.4/5 亚变体的强烈体液反应,mRNA-1273 和 BNT162b2 受体之间的刺突结合抗体水平和中和滴度相当。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明两种 mRNA 疫苗 在疗养院居民中引发类似强大的体液免疫,支持在加强策略中互换使用。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现表明,及时的加强注射,而不是产品选择,可能是保护这一高危、免疫衰老人群的关键决定因素。
局限与证据强度
该研究的样本量较小且缺乏随机性限制了因果推断和普遍性,需要进行适度的置信度评估。

Comparative immunogenicity of mRNA-1273 and BNT162b2 SARS-CoV-2 bivalent booster vaccines in US nursing home residents.

Authors: Olajide J. Olagunju, Oladayo A. Oyebanji, Debbie Keresztesy, Dennis Wilk, Evan Dickerson, Tiffany Wallace, Laurel Holland, Michael C Payne, Ellen See, Chia Jung Li
Original abstract (English)
Nursing home residents (NHRs) remain among the most vulnerable to severe outcomes from SARS-CoV-2 infection. While mRNA-1273 (Spikevax, Moderna) and BNT162b2 (Comirnaty, Pfizer-BioNTech) vaccines are widely used in this population, comparative data on their immunogenicity, particularly after bivalent boosters, remain limited. We conducted a longitudinal immunologic evaluation of U.S. NHRs who received either the mRNA-1273 or BNT162b2 bivalent vaccine. Serum samples were collected 10-30 d post-vaccination. Anti-spike IgG levels were measured using a Luminex bead-based assay, and neutralizing titers were assessed via pseudovirus neutralization. Comparative analyses of the titer distributions were performed using two-sided Wilcoxon rank-sum tests. Both vaccine groups demonstrated strong humoral responses to the ancestral Wuhan strain and Omicron BA.4/5 subvariants. Spike-binding antibody levels and neutralization titers were comparable between the mRNA -1273 and BNT162b2 vaccine formulations. Conclusions: Both mRNA-1273 and BNT162b2 mRNA vaccines elicit similarly robust humoral immunity in NHRs, supporting their interchangeable use in booster strategies. Our findings underscore the importance of timely booster administration over exact product selection in protecting this high-risk, immunosenescent population.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Nursing home residents remain highly vulnerable to severe outcomes from SARS-CoV-2 infection, yet comparative immunogenicity data for mRNA-1273 and BNT162b2 bivalent booster vaccines in this population are limited.
Design and population
A longitudinal immunologic evaluation was conducted among U.S. nursing home residents who received either the mRNA-1273 or BNT162b2 bivalent vaccine.
Core methods
Serum samples were collected 10–30 days post-vaccination, and anti-spike IgG levels were measured using a Luminex bead-based assay while neutralizing titers were assessed via pseudovirus neutralization. Comparative analyses of titer distributions were performed using two-sided Wilcoxon rank-sum tests.
Main results
Both vaccine groups demonstrated strong humoral responses to the ancestral Wuhan strain and Omicron BA.4/5 subvariants, with spike-binding antibody levels and neutralization titers comparable between mRNA-1273 and BNT162b2 recipients.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these findings as evidence that both mRNA vaccines elicit similarly robust humoral immunity in nursing home residents, supporting interchangeable use in booster strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest that timely booster administration, rather than product selection, may be the key determinant of protection in this high-risk, immunosenescent population.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s small sample size and lack of randomization limit causal inference and generalizability, warranting a moderate confidence assessment.

恒河猴和人类抑制 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 复制所需的 nirmatrelvir 和 molnupiravir 血浆浓度不同

Plasma concentrations of nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir required for inhibition of SARS-CoV-2 replication differ between rhesus macaques and humans
作者： Ugo Avila-Ponce de Leon, Shadisadat Esmaeili, Katherine Owens, Joshua T. Schiffer, Ugo Avila-Ponce de León
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addressed whether non-human primate (NHP) infection models can precisely inform optimal dosing of antiviral drugs in humans during the COVID-19 pandemic. It highlighted uncertainty in translating NHP-derived antiviral efficacy to human dosing requirements for SARS-CoV-2.
设计与对象
该研究将数学模型应用于 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的恒河猴 (RM) 的连续病毒载量数据,这些恒河猴要么未经治疗,要么用 nirmatrelvir/ritonavir、molnupiravir 或两种药物治疗。这些模型之前是使用人体临床试验中的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 病毒载量建立和验证的。
核心方法
数学模型适合人类临床试验中的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 病毒载量,以估计在人类中抑制病毒复制 50% 所需的血浆药物浓度。然后将这些模型应用于 抗病毒药物 感染的恒河猴的病毒载量数据,以估计 抗病毒药物 功效和所需的药物浓度。
主要结果
与鼻道相比,尼马瑞韦和莫努匹拉韦的等效血浆药物浓度对应于肺部中更高的 抗病毒药物 效力。nirmatrelvir 抗病毒药物 在鼻道中的平均疗效估计为 30%,在肺中的平均疗效为 46%,由于血浆药物半衰期较短,低于人体中估计的 82% 疗效。
解释与创新
作者解释说,基于 NHP 病毒载量模型的人体剂量优化受到药代动力学、药效学和病毒学特征的药物特异性差异的限制。他们得出的结论是,人体 1 期和 2 期试验数据更适合确定最佳剂量。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果提醒人们,切勿直接将NHP来源的抗病毒药物药效外推至人类给药剂量,并强调需要人类临床试验数据来指导严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2抗病毒药物的给药策略。这对临床前研究设计及将抗病毒药物药效从动物模型转化为临床实践具有重要意义。
局限与证据强度
该研究承认,基于 NHP 模型的人体剂量优化受到药代动力学、药效学和病毒学特征的药物特异性差异的限制。由于依赖模型且 NHP 数据对人类的普遍性有限,证据强度中等。

Plasma concentrations of nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir required for inhibition of SARS-CoV-2 replication differ between rhesus macaques and humans

Authors: Ugo Avila-Ponce de Leon, Shadisadat Esmaeili, Katherine Owens, Joshua T. Schiffer, Ugo Avila-Ponce de León
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Early during the COVID-19 pandemic, non-human primate (NHP) infection models emerged as highly useful tools for preclinical screening of antiviral drugs. However, it is uncertain whether NHP models can be used to precisely inform optimal dosing in humans. We previously established and validated mathematical models, which were fit to SARS-CoV-2 viral loads from human clinical trials. These models identified that plasma drug concentrations required to inhibit viral replication by 50% in humans ( in vivo EC50) differ substantially from in vitro EC50 estimates in cell culture systems. Here, we apply models to sequential viral load data from SARS-CoV-2 infected rhesus macaques (RMs) that were untreated or treated with nirmatrelvir/ritonavir, molnupiravir, or both drugs. We identify that equivalent plasma drug concentrations correspond to greater antiviral potency in the lungs compared with nasal passages for nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir. Average nirmatrelvir antiviral efficacy in RM (30% in nasal passages and 46% in the lungs) was estimated to be less than in humans (82%) due to shorter plasma drug half-life. Molnupiravir efficacy in RMs (95% in nasal and 99% in the lungs) is estimated to be similar to efficacy in humans against omicron variants. Our model estimates that 10-fold higher plasma nirmatelvir concentrations are needed in humans versus RMs to achieve 50% reduction in viral replication, whereas 20-fold lower plasma molnupiravir concentrations are needed. Our results suggest that dose optimization in humans based on modeling of NHP viral loads is limited by drug-specific differences in pharmacokinetic, pharmacodynamic, and virologic profiles, and that data from human phase 1 and 2 trials are better suited for this task.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed whether non-human primate (NHP) infection models can precisely inform optimal dosing of antiviral drugs in humans during the COVID-19 pandemic. It highlighted uncertainty in translating NHP-derived antiviral efficacy to human dosing requirements for SARS-CoV-2.
Design and population
The study applied mathematical models to sequential viral load data from SARS-CoV-2 infected rhesus macaques (RMs) that were either untreated or treated with nirmatrelvir/ritonavir, molnupiravir, or both drugs. The models were previously established and validated using SARS-CoV-2 viral loads from human clinical trials.
Core methods
Mathematical models were fit to SARS-CoV-2 viral loads from human clinical trials to estimate plasma drug concentrations required to inhibit viral replication by 50% in humans. These models were then applied to viral load data from SARS-CoV-2 infected rhesus macaques to estimate antiviral efficacy and required drug concentrations.
Main results
Equivalent plasma drug concentrations corresponded to greater antiviral potency in the lungs compared with nasal passages for both nirmatrelvir and molnupiravir. Average nirmatrelvir antiviral efficacy in RMs was estimated at 30% in nasal passages and 46% in the lungs, lower than the 82% efficacy estimated in humans due to a shorter plasma drug half-life.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that dose optimization in humans based on NHP viral load modeling is limited by drug-specific differences in pharmacokinetic, pharmacodynamic, and virologic profiles. They concluded that human phase 1 and 2 trial data are better suited for determining optimal dosing.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings caution against directly extrapolating NHP-derived antiviral efficacy to human dosing, emphasizing the need for human trial data to inform drug dosing strategies for SARS-CoV-2. This has implications for the design of preclinical studies and the translation of antiviral efficacy from animal models to clinical practice.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study acknowledges that dose optimization in humans based on NHP modeling is constrained by drug-specific differences in pharmacokinetic, pharmacodynamic, and virologic profiles. The evidence strength is moderate due to reliance on modeling and the limited generalizability of NHP data to humans.

The impact of expanded adolescent vaccination against COVID-19 depends on the epidemic status: a mathematical modelling study

The impact of expanded adolescent vaccination against COVID-19 depends on the epidemic status: a mathematical modelling study
作者： Anna G Fairweather, Ben Swallow, Robyn M. Stuart, Cliff C Kerr, Chris Bonell, Russell Viner, Panovska-Griffiths, J., Jasmina Panovska‐Griffiths, Fairweather, A. G., Stuart, R. M.
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估青少年 疫苗接种 对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 的影响如何根据流行状况而变化,特别是在 2021 年末的 Omicron 浪潮和 2022 年的流行阶段。
设计与对象
英格兰使用 Covasim 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 模型进行的一项数学模型研究评估了两个不同流行时期仅为 18 岁以上青少年接种疫苗与扩大到 12 岁以上青少年的影响:2021 年末出现大规模 Omicron 波,2022 年疫情处于流行阶段。
核心方法
该研究使用 Covasim 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 模型来模拟不同 疫苗 摄取和发病时间的影响,预测不同 疫苗 策略下每日新感染、住院和死亡的数量。
主要结果
During the BA.1 Omicron wave in late 2021, expanded adolescent vaccination averted 3,000,000 cases across all ages and 1,010,000 infections in the vaccinated cohort within 2-6 months. In 2022, when the Omicron epidemic was at an endemic stage, additional adolescent vaccination did not significantly reduce the overall burden of COVID-19 or within the vaccinated cohort.
解释与创新
作者解释说,青少年 疫苗接种 的有效性取决于实施的时机和速度,以及其他干预措施和流行状况,并强调不应将其视为独立策略。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对青少年 疫苗接种 的公共卫生策略应根据当前的流行状况、发病率 和 患病率 进行调整,确保在高 传播 期间产生最佳影响。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于数学模型,可能无法完全捕捉现实世界的复杂性,而且结果仅针对英格兰的情况,限制了普遍性。由于这些因素,证据的强度中等。

The impact of expanded adolescent vaccination against COVID-19 depends on the epidemic status: a mathematical modelling study

Authors: Anna G Fairweather, Ben Swallow, Robyn M. Stuart, Cliff C Kerr, Chris Bonell, Russell Viner, Panovska-Griffiths, J., Jasmina Panovska‐Griffiths, Fairweather, A. G., Stuart, R. M.
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background/ObjectivesWe evaluated the impact of the COVID-19 adolescent vaccination in England at two different epidemic points: the autumn (August-November) 2021, in the presence of a large Omicron epidemic wave, and the autumn (August-November) 2022, when the subsequent Omicron epidemic was at an endemic stage. MethodsUsing the Covasim SARS-CoV-2 model for England, under varying vaccine uptake and onset time, we evaluated the impact of a)vaccinating 18+ only versus additional 12+ vaccination from the autumn 2021; and b)the current immunisation strategy at the time versus additional 12+ vaccination from September 2022, projecting the number of new daily SARS-CoV-2 infections, hospitalisations and deaths. ResultsIn presence of the BA.1 Omicron wave in late 2021, the expanded adolescent vaccination averted 3,000,000 cases across all-ages, 1,010,000 SARS-CoV-2 infections in the period 2-6 months from vaccine onset in the vaccinated cohort. During the Omicron waves in 2022, additional adolescents vaccination did not significantly reduce the COVID-19 burden in the entire population, nor within the vaccinated cohort. ConclusionsOur findings highlight that adolescent vaccination impact depends on the timing/speed of implementation, other present intervention strategies, and the status of the epidemic at the time and it should not be considered as a stand-alone immunisation strategy. HighlightsO_LIFirst study to compare the impact of expanding adolescent COVID-19 vaccination at two different epidemic points: one when a large epidemic is spreading and another at a more endemic stage of the epidemic. C_LIO_LIIn autumn 2021, in the presence of the newly emerging Omicron variant, adolescent vaccination was necessary to curb infections in both the adolescent cohort and the overall population. C_LIO_LIEarlier implementation at sufficient coverage was more effective in reducing the number of infections at the peak of the Omicron wave, and getting ahead of the epidemic spread. C_LIO_LIIn autumn 2022, when the Omicron epidemic was at an endemic stage, adolescent vaccination was not necessary to curb infections in both the adolescent cohort and the overall population. C_LIO_LIOur work shows that implementation, timing and speed of an adolescent immunisation programmes should be considered in conjunction with the epidemic status, incidence and prevalence of the epidemic at that time.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate how the impact of adolescent vaccination against SARS-CoV-2 varies depending on the epidemic status, specifically during the Omicron wave in late 2021 and its endemic phase in 2022.
Design and population
A mathematical modelling study using the Covasim SARS-CoV-2 model for England assessed the impact of vaccinating 18+ only versus expanding to 12+ adolescents during two different epidemic periods: late 2021 with a large Omicron wave and 2022 when the epidemic was at an endemic stage.
Core methods
The study used the Covasim SARS-CoV-2 model to simulate the effects of varying vaccine uptake and onset times, projecting the number of new daily infections, hospitalisations, and deaths under different vaccination strategies.
Main results
During the BA.1 Omicron wave in late 2021, expanded adolescent vaccination averted 3,000,000 cases across all ages and 1,010,000 infections in the vaccinated cohort within 2-6 months. In 2022, when the Omicron epidemic was at an endemic stage, additional adolescent vaccination did not significantly reduce the overall burden of COVID-19 or within the vaccinated cohort.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that the effectiveness of adolescent vaccination depends on the timing and speed of implementation, alongside other interventions and the epidemic status, and emphasized that it should not be considered as a standalone strategy.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that public health strategies for adolescent vaccination should be tailored to the current epidemic status, incidence, and prevalence, ensuring optimal impact during periods of high transmission.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on mathematical modelling, which may not fully capture real-world complexities, and the results are specific to England's context, limiting generalisability. The evidence is moderate in strength due to these factors.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 原型及其(子)变体的 NTD 抗体中和和免疫逃逸的结构和机制图谱。

A structural and mechanistic atlas of NTD antibody neutralization and immune escape across SARS-CoV-2 prototype and its (sub-)variants.
作者： Jianjie Zhou, Wenyu Li, Xiaoyun Wang, Junqing Sun, Shuxin Guo, Xiaoyu Rong, Zhou Tong, Lianpan Dai, William Jun Liu, Jianxun Qi
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突 (S) 的 N 端结构域 (NTD) 是一个关键的抗体靶点,但其表位组织、中和机制和免疫逃避策略仍未完全解决。在这里,我们将 NTD 抗体分为九个空间不同的类别(指定为 NTD-1 至 NTD-9),包括此处定义的神秘表位 (NTD-8)。机制研究表明,NTD-5 和 NTD-9 抗体通过诱导 S1 脱落来中和,从而将该机制扩展到选定的 NTD 定向抗体。格式分析显示,虽然大多数 NTD 抗体需要二价,但来自 NTD-3、NTD-5 和 NTD-9 的选定抗体保留了 Fab 形式的中和活性。对原型、Delta 和 17 个 Omicron 亚变体的 41 种抗体进行分析,定义了表位解析的逃逸景观,并能够剖析三种聚合逃逸策略:接触残基破坏、聚糖屏蔽和构象重塑。值得注意的是,KP.3.1.1 亚变体使用双重逃逸机制,其中 ΔS31 引入 N30 糖基化并显着重塑 S27-R34 区域,从而破坏 NTD-5 和 NTD-9 抗体的识别。这些发现为合理的 疫苗 和抗抗原漂移的抗体设计提供了结构和机制框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对针对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 刺突蛋白 N 端结构域 (NTD) 的抗体如何中和病毒以及跨变体如何发生免疫逃逸的不完全理解。它询问是否可以对 NTD 抗体的表位组织和中和机制进行系统分类和机械解释,为 疫苗 和抗体设计提供信息。
设计与对象
The research classifies NTD antibodies into nine spatially distinct classes and profiles 41 antibodies across the 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 prototype,Delta,and 17 Omicron subvariants.它包括剖析中和和逃避策略的机制研究。
核心方法
通过空间表位作图定义抗体类别,并通过结构和功能测定研究中和机制。通过评估跨变体(包括 KP.3.1.1 亚变体)的抗体识别来描绘逃逸景观。
主要结果
鉴定出九种 NTD 抗体类别(NTD-1 至 NTD-9),包括一个隐藏表位 (NTD-8)。NTD-5 和 NTD-9 抗体通过诱导 S1 脱落来中和,并且来自 NTD-3、NTD-5 和 NTD-9 的选定抗体以 Fab 形式保留活性。KP.3.1.1 亚变体通过 ΔS31 采用双重逃逸,引入 N30 糖基化并重塑 S27-R34 区域,破坏 NTD-5 和 NTD-9 识别。
解释与创新
The work provides an epitope-resolved escape landscape and reveals three convergent evasion strategies: contact residue disruption,glycan shielding,and conformational remodeling.它介绍了用于设计 疫苗 和抗抗原漂移的抗体的结构和机制框架。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以指导下一代 疫苗 和单克隆抗体的开发,这些抗体具有更高的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 免疫逃逸恢复能力,特别是针对 Omicron 亚变体。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于体外结构和功能分析,不涉及体内功效、临床结果或群体水平 免疫应答。由于仅限摘要范围且缺乏直接的临床验证,证据强度中等。

A structural and mechanistic atlas of NTD antibody neutralization and immune escape across SARS-CoV-2 prototype and its (sub-)variants.

Authors: Jianjie Zhou, Wenyu Li, Xiaoyun Wang, Junqing Sun, Shuxin Guo, Xiaoyu Rong, Zhou Tong, Lianpan Dai, William Jun Liu, Jianxun Qi
Original abstract (English)
The N-terminal domain (NTD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike (S) is a critical antibody target, yet its epitope organization, neutralization mechanisms, and immune evasion strategies remain incompletely resolved. Here, we classify NTD antibodies into nine spatially distinct classes (designated as NTD-1 to NTD-9), including a cryptic epitope defined here (NTD-8). Mechanistic studies reveal that NTD-5 and NTD-9 antibodies neutralize by inducing S1 shedding, thereby extending this mechanism to selected NTD-directed antibodies. Format profiling shows that while most NTD antibodies require bivalency, selected antibodies from NTD-3, NTD-5, and NTD-9 retain neutralizing activity in Fab form. Profiling 41 antibodies across prototype, Delta, and 17 Omicron subvariants defines an epitope-resolved escape landscape and enables dissection of three convergent evasion strategies: contact residue disruption, glycan shielding, and conformational remodeling. Notably, the KP.3.1.1 subvariant uses a dual escape mechanism in which ∆S31 introduces N30 glycosylation and substantially remodels the S27-R34 region, undermining recognition by both NTD-5 and NTD-9 antibodies. These findings provide a structural and mechanistic framework for rational vaccine and antibody design resilient to antigenic drift.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the incomplete understanding of how antibodies targeting the N-terminal domain (NTD) of the SARS-CoV-2 spike protein neutralize the virus and how immune escape occurs across variants. It asks whether the epitope organization and neutralization mechanisms of NTD antibodies can be systematically classified and mechanistically explained to inform vaccine and antibody design.
Design and population
The research classifies NTD antibodies into nine spatially distinct classes and profiles 41 antibodies across the SARS-CoV-2 prototype, Delta, and 17 Omicron subvariants. It includes mechanistic studies to dissect neutralization and escape strategies.
Core methods
Antibody classes were defined by spatial epitope mapping, and neutralization mechanisms were investigated through structural and functional assays. Escape landscapes were profiled by assessing antibody recognition across variants, including the KP.3.1.1 subvariant.
Main results
Nine NTD antibody classes (NTD-1 to NTD-9) were identified, including a cryptic epitope (NTD-8). NTD-5 and NTD-9 antibodies neutralize by inducing S1 shedding, and selected antibodies from NTD-3, NTD-5, and NTD-9 retain activity in Fab form. The KP.3.1.1 subvariant employs dual escape via ∆S31 introducing N30 glycosylation and remodeling the S27-R34 region, undermining NTD-5 and NTD-9 recognition.
Interpretation and novelty
The work provides an epitope-resolved escape landscape and reveals three convergent evasion strategies: contact residue disruption, glycan shielding, and conformational remodeling. It introduces a structural and mechanistic framework for designing vaccines and antibodies resilient to antigenic drift.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can guide the development of next-generation vaccines and monoclonal antibodies with improved resilience to SARS-CoV-2 immune escape, particularly against Omicron subvariants.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to structural and functional profiling in vitro and does not address in vivo efficacy, clinical outcomes, or population-level immune responses. The strength of evidence is moderate due to the abstract-only scope and lack of direct clinical validation.

血清流行率 趋同掩盖了加拿大 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染风险中持续存在的社会经济差异

Seroprevalence convergence masks persistent socioeconomic disparities in SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in Canada
作者： Abdullah Hassan, Emmanuel Issa Nassrallah, David N. Fisman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Socioeconomic disparities in COVID-19 outcomes have been documented, but evidence on inequities in SARS-CoV-2 infection risk remains inconsistent. In Canada, apparent convergence in SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence across socioeconomic strata by late 2022 raised uncertainty about whether inequities in infection risk had diminished during the Omicron period.
设计与对象
本研究分析了 2021 年 4 月至 2023 年 4 月期间收集的加拿大血液服务捐献者的连续横截面 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 血清流行率 数据,按地区物质匮乏五分位数分层(Q1 = 最不匮乏;Q5 = 最匮乏)。
核心方法
血清流行率 使用抗核衣壳抗体测定进行测量。将具有血清回复的动态血清阴性-血清阳性模型拟合到完整的 血清流行率 时间序列,以估计 Omicron 变种出现之前和之后(2022 年 1 月)的五分位特异性感染力。使用基于可能性的标准对允许通过物质剥夺进行差异 Omicron 相关放大的模型进行了比较。
主要结果
在 Omicron 之前的时期,最贫困的五分之一 (Q5) 的感染力比最贫困的五分之一 (Q1;发病率 率比 (IRR):1.71;95% CI:1.60–1.83)高 71%。Omicron 出现后,感染力的相对增幅在最贫困的五分之一中最大(48.5 倍),在最贫困的五分之一中最小(31.8 倍),压缩了相对社会经济梯度(Q5 与 Q1 IRR:1.12;95% CI:1.11-1.14)。尽管存在这种压缩,但在整个 Omicron 时期,最贫困的人群继续经历着更高的绝对感染力。
解释与创新
作者将 血清流行率 中的趋同解释为掩盖了感染力方面持续存在的社会经济不平等,并将其归因于 Omicron 时期感染风险的差异放大,而不是消除了潜在的差异。这项研究提供了新的证据,表明相对 血清流行率 收敛可以掩盖绝对感染风险中持续存在的不平等。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现警告不要将 血清流行率 趋同解释为感染风险差异缩小的证据,并强调需要在社会经济贫困人群中制定有针对性的 血清流行率 和预防策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于地区层面的剥夺措施和基于捐助者的 血清流行率 数据,这些数据可能无法完全捕捉个人层面的社会经济因素或感染史。由于模型依赖性以及血清回复假设和采样框架固有的潜在偏差,证据强度中等。

Seroprevalence convergence masks persistent socioeconomic disparities in SARS-CoV-2 infection risk in Canada

Authors: Abdullah Hassan, Emmanuel Issa Nassrallah, David N. Fisman
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Socioeconomic disparities in COVID-19 outcomes have been widely documented, but evidence regarding inequities in SARS-CoV-2 infection risk remains mixed. In Canada, infection-induced seroprevalence appeared to converge across socioeconomic strata by late 2022, raising questions about whether inequities in infection risk diminished during the Omicron period. AIM: To assess whether apparent convergence in SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence reflects true equity in infection risk or masks persistent socioeconomic disparities in force of infection. METHODS: We analysed serial cross-sectional SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence data (anti-nucleocapsid antibodies) from Canadian Blood Services donors collected between April 2021 and April 2023 and stratified by area-level material deprivation quintile (Q1 = least deprived; Q5 = most deprived). We fitted a dynamic seronegative-seropositive model with sero-reversion to the full seroprevalence time series, estimating quintile-specific forces of infection before and after the emergence of the Omicron variant (January 2022). Models allowing differential Omicron-related amplification by material deprivation were compared using likelihood-based criteria. RESULTS: During the pre-Omicron period, the most deprived quintile (Q5) experienced a 71% higher force of infection than the least deprived (Q1; incidence rate ratio (IRR): 1.71; 95% CI: 1.60-1.83). Following Omicron emergence, force of infection rose markedly in all quintiles. Because the pre-Omicron baseline was lowest in the least-deprived quintile, the relative increase during Omicron was largest there (48.5-fold) and smallest in the most-deprived quintile (31.8-fold), compressing the relative socioeconomic gradient (Q5 vs Q1 IRR: 1.12; 95% CI: 1.11-1.14). Despite this compression in relative terms, the most deprived populations continued to experience higher absolute force of infection throughout the Omicron period. CONCLUSION: Convergence in SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence across socioeconomic strata masked persistent inequalities in force of infection. Dynamic modelling demonstrates that apparent equity is attributable, in our modelling framework, to differential amplification of force of infection during the Omicron period rather than from elimination of underlying socioeconomic disparities.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Socioeconomic disparities in COVID-19 outcomes have been documented, but evidence on inequities in SARS-CoV-2 infection risk remains inconsistent. In Canada, apparent convergence in SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence across socioeconomic strata by late 2022 raised uncertainty about whether inequities in infection risk had diminished during the Omicron period.
Design and population
This study analysed serial cross-sectional SARS-CoV-2 seroprevalence data from Canadian Blood Services donors collected between April 2021 and April 2023, stratified by area-level material deprivation quintile (Q1 = least deprived; Q5 = most deprived).
Core methods
Seroprevalence was measured using anti-nucleocapsid antibody assays. A dynamic seronegative-seropositive model with sero-reversion was fitted to the full seroprevalence time series to estimate quintile-specific forces of infection before and after the emergence of the Omicron variant (January 2022). Models allowing differential Omicron-related amplification by material deprivation were compared using likelihood-based criteria.
Main results
During the pre-Omicron period, the most deprived quintile (Q5) experienced a 71% higher force of infection than the least deprived (Q1; incidence rate ratio (IRR): 1.71; 95% CI: 1.60–1.83). Following Omicron emergence, the relative increase in force of infection was largest in the least-deprived quintile (48.5-fold) and smallest in the most-deprived quintile (31.8-fold), compressing the relative socioeconomic gradient (Q5 vs Q1 IRR: 1.12; 95% CI: 1.11–1.14). Despite this compression, the most deprived populations continued to experience higher absolute force of infection throughout the Omicron period.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the convergence in seroprevalence as masking persistent socioeconomic inequalities in force of infection, attributing this to differential amplification of infection risk during the Omicron period rather than elimination of underlying disparities. This study provides novel evidence that relative seroprevalence convergence can obscure ongoing inequities in absolute infection risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings caution against interpreting seroprevalence convergence as evidence of reduced infection risk disparities and highlight the need for targeted surveillance and prevention strategies in socioeconomically deprived populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on area-level deprivation measures and donor-based seroprevalence data, which may not fully capture individual-level socioeconomic factors or infection histories. The evidence strength is moderate due to model dependence and potential biases inherent in sero-reversion assumptions and sampling frames.

Persistent humoral, cellular, and transcriptional signatures associated with protective immunity against SARS-CoV-2 observed 18-months after COVID-19 vaccinations during the Omicron wave in Pakistan

Persistent humoral, cellular, and transcriptional signatures associated with protective immunity against SARS-CoV-2 observed 18-months after COVID-19 vaccinations during the Omicron wave in Pakistan
作者： Fizza Fatima, Maliha Yameen, Javaria Ashraf, Kiran Iqbal Masood, Shama Qaiser, Junaid Iqbal, Rabia Hussain, Zulfiqar Bhutta, Zahra Hasan
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了人们对 疫苗 诱导的 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 免疫力持久性的担忧,特别是在巴基斯坦等低收入和中等收入国家,这些国家在 Omicron 浪潮期间加强剂的可用性有限。
设计与对象
The research examined a population in Pakistan that had received different COVID-19 vaccines (inactivated, mRNA, and vector types) and evaluated immune responses approximately 18 months after vaccination.
核心方法
该研究通过 IgG 抗体水平评估体液免疫,通过 T 细胞对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 抗原的反应性评估细胞免疫,同时通过 宿主 血液转录分析来识别与 疫苗 反应相关的途径变化。
主要结果
体液和细胞 免疫应答 表明对 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 具有持续的保护作用,转录谱显示细胞信号传导和蛋白质合成途径上调,与 疫苗 相关的变化一致。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证据,表明混合免疫(疫苗接种 和自然感染相结合)可能有助于巴基斯坦的持续保护,尽管加强剂的可用性有限,并指出这是首批在转录水平上表征大流行后免疫的研究之一。
科研与公卫意义
结果表明,混合免疫可能在资源匮乏的环境中提供了长期保护,可能为类似环境下的 疫苗接种 策略和 监测 方法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据仅限于抽象级别的数据,缺乏详细的方法论、人口统计或统计报告,这限制了结论的强度和普遍性。

Persistent humoral, cellular, and transcriptional signatures associated with protective immunity against SARS-CoV-2 observed 18-months after COVID-19 vaccinations during the Omicron wave in Pakistan

Authors: Fizza Fatima, Maliha Yameen, Javaria Ashraf, Kiran Iqbal Masood, Shama Qaiser, Junaid Iqbal, Rabia Hussain, Zulfiqar Bhutta, Zahra Hasan
Original abstract (English)
Abstract During the COVID-19 pandemic the population experienced waves of SARS-CoV-2 infections as well as vaccinations. Concern with longer term effectiveness of vaccinations led to booster vaccinations across global population, more high resource settings but not in low middle income countries such as Pakistan. We studied a population which received different COVID-19 vaccines (inactivated, mRNA and vector types) and were tested this one year and a half afterwards. Humoral responses determined through IgG antibody levels and cellular immunity studied through T cell reactivity to SARS-CoV-2 antigens all indicated persistent protection against the coronavirus. Through a comparison with a pre-pandemic cohort, host blood transcriptional profiles of the post-vaccination cohort showed upregulated cellular signaling and protein synthesis pathways reflective of vaccine associated changes. Additionally, cellular stress associated responses were apparent which are consistent with viral infection signatures as well as vaccination effects. Our results reflect an effect likely as a combination of COVID-19 vaccination and immune boosting due to SARS-CoV-2 transmission leading to trained immunity in the absence of COVID-19 boosters. It’s the first work of its kind- very limited information globally on post-pandemic immunity. It shows at the transcriptional level how immune activation is maintained, stress pathways and cellular senescence. These data show hybrid immunity likely gave longer-term protection observed in Pakistan despite limited COVID-19 vaccination availability, with relatively low morbidity and mortality.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed concerns about the durability of vaccine-induced immunity against SARS-CoV-2, particularly in low- and middle-income countries such as Pakistan where booster availability was limited during the Omicron wave.
Design and population
The research examined a population in Pakistan that had received different COVID-19 vaccines (inactivated, mRNA, and vector types) and evaluated immune responses approximately 18 months after vaccination.
Core methods
The study assessed humoral immunity via IgG antibody levels and cellular immunity through T cell reactivity to SARS-CoV-2 antigens, alongside host blood transcriptional profiling to identify pathway changes associated with vaccine responses.
Main results
Humoral and cellular immune responses indicated persistent protection against SARS-CoV-2, and transcriptional profiles showed upregulated cellular signaling and protein synthesis pathways consistent with vaccine-associated changes.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as evidence that hybrid immunity—combining vaccination and natural infection—likely contributed to sustained protection in Pakistan despite limited booster availability, and noted this was among the first studies to characterize post-pandemic immunity at the transcriptional level.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that hybrid immunity may have provided longer-term protection in low-resource settings, potentially informing vaccination strategies and surveillance approaches in similar contexts.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is limited to abstract-level data and lacks detailed methodological, demographic, or statistical reporting, which constrains the strength of the conclusions and generalizability.

Healthy vaccinee effect in the evaluation of updated COVID-19 vaccines in elderly populations

Healthy vaccinee effect in the evaluation of updated COVID-19 vaccines in elderly populations
作者： Johan Lyth, Armin Spreco, Jorma Hinkula, Olof Björneld, Olle Eriksson, Dennis Nordvall, Örjan Dahlström, Elin A. Gursky, Thomas Schön, Toomas Timpka
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addresses whether updated COVID-19 vaccines adapted to the Omicron JN.1 lineage provide protection against hospitalization in elderly populations, where unmeasured frailty may bias vaccine effectiveness estimates.
设计与对象
A cohort study was conducted in Sweden among individuals aged 65 years or older (N = 245,696) between October 1, 2024, and March 31, 2025, to evaluate vaccine effectiveness against COVID-19-related hospitalization.
核心方法
使用观察性 队列研究 评估 疫苗 的有效性,并使用阴性对照结果(全因 死亡率)来检测健康疫苗接种者的效果,并计算风险比以比较接种疫苗组和未接种疫苗组之间的结果。
主要结果
疫苗 整体有效性为 75%(95% CI 70%–79%),既往于 2023-2024 年接种过者有效性为 84%(95% CI 80%–87%),而未接种者为 65%(95% CI 34%–82%)。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为由于向体弱老年人提供的卫生服务不足而导致选择效应的证据,强调需要在观察性 疫苗 有效性研究中考虑健康疫苗接种者的影响。
科研与公卫意义
The results suggest that updated COVID-19 vaccines remain effective in preventing hospitalization in elderly populations but may overestimate protection if frailty-related access disparities are not addressed in surveillance.
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其观察设计、未测量的虚弱带来的潜在残留混杂因素以及对行政数据的依赖的限制,这可能会影响 疫苗 有效性估计的准确性和普遍性。

Healthy vaccinee effect in the evaluation of updated COVID-19 vaccines in elderly populations

Authors: Johan Lyth, Armin Spreco, Jorma Hinkula, Olof Björneld, Olle Eriksson, Dennis Nordvall, Örjan Dahlström, Elin A. Gursky, Thomas Schön, Toomas Timpka
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Established determinants of health in the elderly help guide routines for indicated vaccine administration, while unmeasured frailty may limit vaccine access. We evaluate the performance of the 2024-2025 COVID-19 vaccine adapted to the Omicron JN.1 lineage in a Swedish population aged ≥65 years ( N = 245 696). Vaccine effectiveness (VE) on COVID-19-related hospitalization and a negative control outcome (NCO; all-cause mortality) are assessed in various cohorts between October 1, 2024 to March 31, 2025. The VE was 75% (95% CI 70%–79%) overall, and 84% (95% CI 80%–87%) and 65% (95% CI 34%–82%) in individuals with and without vaccination with the prior updated COVID-19 vaccine in 2023-2024, respectively. The NCO in individuals exposed to the study vaccine in 2024-2025 was half of that seen in those not exposed to the vaccine (HR 0.43 [95% CI 0.40–0.45]). Removing individuals hospitalized with COVID-19 from this population did not change the difference in NCO (HR 0.43 [95% CI 0.41-0.46]). These findings suggest the presence of selection effects arising from under-provision of health services to elderly individuals with frailty. The healthy vaccinee effect should be considered in observational studies of the effectiveness of updated COVID-19 vaccines in elderly populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses whether updated COVID-19 vaccines adapted to the Omicron JN.1 lineage provide protection against hospitalization in elderly populations, where unmeasured frailty may bias vaccine effectiveness estimates.
Design and population
A cohort study was conducted in Sweden among individuals aged 65 years or older (N = 245,696) between October 1, 2024, and March 31, 2025, to evaluate vaccine effectiveness against COVID-19-related hospitalization.
Core methods
Vaccine effectiveness was assessed using observational cohorts, with a negative control outcome (all-cause mortality) to detect healthy vaccinee effects, and hazard ratios were calculated to compare outcomes between vaccinated and unvaccinated groups.
Main results
The vaccine effectiveness was 75% (95% CI 70%–79%) overall, with higher effectiveness in individuals previously vaccinated in 2023–2024 (84%, 95% CI 80%–87%) compared to those without prior vaccination (65%, 95% CI 34%–82%).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as evidence of selection effects due to under-provision of health services to frail elderly individuals, highlighting the need to account for the healthy vaccinee effect in observational vaccine effectiveness studies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results suggest that updated COVID-19 vaccines remain effective in preventing hospitalization in elderly populations but may overestimate protection if frailty-related access disparities are not addressed in surveillance.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its observational design, potential residual confounding from unmeasured frailty, and reliance on administrative data, which may affect the accuracy of vaccine effectiveness estimates and generalizability.

Epidemiology and mortality among hospital-acquired cases of COVID-19 in the pre-Omicron and Omicron periods: A data-linkage study in Victoria, Australia.

Epidemiology and mortality among hospital-acquired cases of COVID-19 in the pre-Omicron and Omicron periods: A data-linkage study in Victoria, Australia.
作者： Lyn-Li Lim, Stephanie K Tanamas, Suresh Karunappan, Leon J Worth, Ann Bull, Elizabeth J Robinson, N Deborah Friedman
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study addressed the public-health problem of hospital-acquired COVID-19 by asking why in-hospital infections carry higher risks than community-acquired cases and whether this changed between pre-Omicron and Omicron periods.
设计与对象
A retrospective cohort study was conducted in Victoria, Australia, linking state surveillance systems to identify COVID-19 hospitalisations from January 2021 to June 2024, classifying episodes as hospital-acquired if the first positive test occurred at least eight days after admission.
核心方法
使用关联的 监测 数据,对 Omicron 之前和 Omicron 期间医院获得性和非医院获得性病例的年龄、性别、重症监护病房入院情况和 30 天 死亡率 进行比较。
主要结果
在 6,485 例医院获得性发作和 62,670 例非医院获得性发作中,中位年龄分别为 77.8 岁和 Omicron 之前的 77.6 岁,Omicron 期间分别为 52.6 岁和 73.1 岁;Omicron 下每月医院获得性病例上升至 21%,医院获得性病例的病死率较高,Omicron 前的 发病率 比率为 4.49,Omicron 期间为 1.62。
解释与创新
The authors concluded that hospital-acquired COVID-19 remains clinically significant, disproportionately affecting older patients and carrying higher mortality than non-hospital-acquired infection, highlighting the need for strengthened infection prevention.
科研与公卫意义
The findings inform hospital infection prevention strategies by quantifying the excess mortality risk of hospital-acquired COVID-19 and showing its persistence into the Omicron era.
局限与证据强度
该研究的回顾性设计和对 监测 数据的依赖限制了因果推断和普遍性,因此对其结论有一定的信心。

Epidemiology and mortality among hospital-acquired cases of COVID-19 in the pre-Omicron and Omicron periods: A data-linkage study in Victoria, Australia.

Authors: Lyn-Li Lim, Stephanie K Tanamas, Suresh Karunappan, Leon J Worth, Ann Bull, Elizabeth J Robinson, N Deborah Friedman
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: The aim of this study was to compare the epidemiology and mortality of hospital-acquired versus non-hospital-acquired COVID-19 infections among hospitalised patients. METHODS: We conducted a retrospective cohort study of COVID‐19 hospitalisations in Victoria, Australia (January 2021-June 2024), using linked state surveillance systems. Episodes were classified as hospital acquired if the first positive test occurred ≥8 days post admission. Age, sex, intensive care unit admission and 30‐day mortality were compared between hospital-acquired and non‐hospital-acquired cases across pre‐Omicron and Omicron periods. RESULTS: Of 6,485 hospital-acquired and 62,670 non-hospital-acquired episodes, median age for hospital-acquired versus non-hospital-acquired cases was 77.8 versus 77.6 years before Omicron and 52.6 versus 73.1 years during Omicron. Monthly proportion of hospital-acquired cases increased during Omicron (range: 1% to 21%). Case fatality rates were higher in hospital-acquired than in non-hospital-acquired cases, with a larger difference before the Omicron (incidence rate ratio: 4.49, 95% confidence interval: 2.02, 9.99) than during the Omicron period (incidence rate ratio: 1.62, 95% confidence interval: 1.44, 1.82). Intensive care unit admission was associated with 30-day mortality for hospital-acquired (adjusted odds ratio: 4.4, 95% confidence interval: 2.3-8.4) and non‐hospital-acquired cases (adjusted odds ratio: 4.0, 95% confidence interval: 3.5-4.7). CONCLUSIONS: Hospital-acquired COVID-19 infections remain significant, associated with higher mortality rates than non-hospital-acquired infection and disproportionately affecting older patients. IMPLICATIONS FOR PUBLIC HEALTH: Our findings underscore the need for robust hospital COVID-19 infection prevention strategies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the public-health problem of hospital-acquired COVID-19 by asking why in-hospital infections carry higher risks than community-acquired cases and whether this changed between pre-Omicron and Omicron periods.
Design and population
A retrospective cohort study was conducted in Victoria, Australia, linking state surveillance systems to identify COVID-19 hospitalisations from January 2021 to June 2024, classifying episodes as hospital-acquired if the first positive test occurred at least eight days after admission.
Core methods
Age, sex, intensive care unit admission and 30-day mortality were compared between hospital-acquired and non-hospital-acquired cases across pre-Omicron and Omicron periods using linked surveillance data.
Main results
Among 6,485 hospital-acquired and 62,670 non-hospital-acquired episodes, median age was 77.8 versus 77.6 years before Omicron and 52.6 versus 73.1 years during Omicron; monthly hospital-acquired cases rose to 21% under Omicron, and case fatality rates were higher for hospital-acquired cases with incidence rate ratios of 4.49 pre-Omicron and 1.62 during Omicron.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that hospital-acquired COVID-19 remains clinically significant, disproportionately affecting older patients and carrying higher mortality than non-hospital-acquired infection, highlighting the need for strengthened infection prevention.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform hospital infection prevention strategies by quantifying the excess mortality risk of hospital-acquired COVID-19 and showing its persistence into the Omicron era.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study’s retrospective design and reliance on surveillance data limit causal inference and generalisability, warranting moderate confidence in its conclusions.

严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 亚变体 JN.1、EG.5.1 和 BA.2.86.1 在免疫学上未接触过的食蟹猴中的致病性。

Pathogenicity of SARS-CoV-2 Omicron subvariants JN.1, EG.5.1, and BA.2.86.1 in immunologically naïve cynomolgus macaques.
作者： Hirohito Ishigaki, Kenichi Otaki, Naoko Kitagawa, Hideaki Ishida, Hiraku Kokubu, Oahida Aktar, Hideji Otani, Masashi Shingai, Hiroshi Kida, Yasushi Itoh
摘要中文翻译自从 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 变种出现以来,相对于早期菌株的发病率降低和 死亡率 已被广泛报道。然而,在大流行后时代占主导地位的近期亚变体的病毒学特征仍有待充分阐明。在本研究中,使用免疫学幼稚的食蟹猴模型研究了 Omicron 亚变体 JN.1、EG.5.1 和 BA.2.86.1 的内在致病性。所有三种变体都在上呼吸道中表现出强劲的复制能力,超过了早期武汉病毒株观察到的病毒滴度。Omicron 亚变体感染导致长时间发烧,并在鼻粘膜中持续病毒脱落至少 7 天,表明对上呼吸道组织的适应增强。尽管病毒复制向性发生了这种转变,但组织学分析表明这些变异体保留了诱发下呼吸道疾病的特征。所有受感染的猕猴都会出现支气管肺炎,其特征是肺泡腔内有细胞渗出物。组织学评分与早期菌株观察到的组织学评分相当。这些发现表明,在缺乏预先存在的免疫力的情况下,Omicron 亚变体的致病性并未完全减弱,因为它们在免疫学上未接触过的猕猴中引起了严重的肺部疾病。此外,它们在上呼吸道的增强传播可能有助于人类中有效的 传播。严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2变种的持续传播强调了持续监测病毒基因变异和维持易感人群保护性免疫力的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了对最初的大流行浪潮后出现的最新 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 Omicron 亚变体(JN.1、EG.5.1、BA.2.86.1)的病毒学特征的不完全理解,尽管有报道称与早期毒株相比,发病率和 死亡率 有所降低。
设计与对象
该研究使用免疫学上幼稚的食蟹猴模型来研究三个 Omicron 亚变体的内在致病性:JN.1、EG.5.1 和 BA.2.86.1。
核心方法
该研究评估了实验感染特定 Omicron 亚变种的猕猴的病毒复制、临床症状、病毒脱落和组织学肺损伤。
主要结果
所有三个 Omicron 亚变种都在上呼吸道中强劲复制,病毒滴度超过了早期的武汉毒株,并引起长时间发烧和持续至少 7 天的鼻腔病毒脱落。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,在缺乏预先存在的免疫力的情况下,Omicron 亚变体的致病性并未完全减弱,因为它们在猕猴中诱发了严重的支气管肺炎,并且它们增强的上呼吸道复制表明了有效的人类 传播 的潜力。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2遗传变异的持续监测的必要性,以及由于具有保留致病潜力的变异体的持续循环而维持易感人群的保护性免疫力的必要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于免疫学上幼稚的非人类灵长类动物模型,该模型可能无法完全概括人类 免疫应答 或疾病严重程度,并且鉴于缺乏人类临床数据,证据强度中等。

Pathogenicity of SARS-CoV-2 Omicron subvariants JN.1, EG.5.1, and BA.2.86.1 in immunologically naïve cynomolgus macaques.

Authors: Hirohito Ishigaki, Kenichi Otaki, Naoko Kitagawa, Hideaki Ishida, Hiraku Kokubu, Oahida Aktar, Hideji Otani, Masashi Shingai, Hiroshi Kida, Yasushi Itoh
Original abstract (English)
Since the emergence of the SARS-CoV-2 Omicron variant, decreased morbidity and mortality relative to early strains have been widely reported. However, the virological characteristics of recent subvariants dominating the post-pandemic era remain to be fully elucidated. In the present study, the intrinsic pathogenicities of the Omicron subvariants JN.1, EG.5.1, and BA.2.86.1 were investigated using an immunologically naïve cynomolgus macaque model. All three variants exhibited robust replication in the upper respiratory tract, surpassing the viral titers observed with the early Wuhan strain. Omicron subvariant infection caused prolonged fever and sustained viral shedding in the nasal mucosa for at least seven days, indicating enhanced adaptation to upper airway tissues. Despite this shift in viral replication tropism, histological analysis showed that these variants retained the characteristics to induce lower respiratory tract disease. All infected macaques developed bronchopneumonia characterized by cellular exudates in the alveolar spaces. The histological scores were comparable to those observed with the early strain. These findings demonstrate that the pathogenicity of the Omicron subvariants has not been fully attenuated in the absence of pre-existing immunity, since they caused severe lung disease in immunologically naïve macaques. Furthermore, their enhanced propagation in the upper airway likely facilitates efficient transmission among humans. The continuous circulation of SARS-CoV-2 variants underscores the necessity of ongoing surveillance of viral gene variations and the maintenance of protective immunity in vulnerable populations.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the incomplete understanding of the virological characteristics of recent SARS-CoV-2 Omicron subvariants (JN.1, EG.5.1, BA.2.86.1) that emerged after the initial pandemic waves, despite reports of reduced morbidity and mortality compared to early strains.
Design and population
The research used an immunologically naïve cynomolgus macaque model to investigate the intrinsic pathogenicity of three Omicron subvariants: JN.1, EG.5.1, and BA.2.86.1.
Core methods
The study assessed viral replication, clinical signs, viral shedding, and histological lung damage in macaques experimentally infected with the specified Omicron subvariants.
Main results
All three Omicron subvariants replicated robustly in the upper respiratory tract, with viral titers exceeding those of the early Wuhan strain, and caused prolonged fever and sustained nasal viral shedding for at least seven days.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that Omicron subvariants have not fully attenuated in pathogenicity in the absence of pre-existing immunity, as they induced severe bronchopneumonia in macaques, and their enhanced upper airway replication suggests potential for efficient human transmission.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the need for continuous surveillance of SARS-CoV-2 genetic variations and the maintenance of protective immunity in vulnerable populations due to the ongoing circulation of variants with retained pathogenic potential.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to an immunologically naïve non-human primate model, which may not fully recapitulate human immune responses or disease severity, and the evidence strength is moderate given the absence of human clinical data.

Effects of State Medicaid Expansions Affecting Parental Eligibility on Children's Healthcare Access and Health During the COVID-19 Pandemic.

Effects of State Medicaid Expansions Affecting Parental Eligibility on Children's Healthcare Access and Health During the COVID-19 Pandemic.
作者： Joel M James, Wehby GL
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
The study aimed to investigate whether pre-pandemic state Medicaid expansions for adults influenced children's healthcare access and utilization, child health, and parental health during the COVID-19 pandemic.
设计与对象
该研究使用了2016-2023年全国儿童健康调查的数据,重点关注收入高达联邦贫困线138%家庭的儿童。
核心方法
采用双重差分模型来估计医疗补助扩张州和非扩张州之间结果的逐年差异。
主要结果
与非扩张州相比,在大流行的第一年或第二年,疾病护理、私人医生或护士以及预防性医疗就诊的常规地点以及 2020/2023 年儿童健康方面的情况均有所下降。相比之下,2021-2023 年扩张州的心理健康专业人士 治疗 和其他专家就诊人数有所增加。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,医疗补助的扩大可能对儿童的医疗保健产生复杂的影响,减少了护理和预防服务的连续性,但增加了大流行期间的心理健康 治疗 和专家就诊。
科研与公卫意义
结果突显了医疗补助扩张对大流行期间儿童医疗保健获取和健康的复杂影响,为未来的政策决策和公共卫生战略提供了信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于观察数据和双重差异模型,可能会受到混杂因素和因果推断的限制。

Effects of State Medicaid Expansions Affecting Parental Eligibility on Children's Healthcare Access and Health During the COVID-19 Pandemic.

Authors: Joel M James, Wehby GL
Original abstract (English)
Objective To examine whether pre-pandemic state Medicaid expansions for adults affected children's healthcare access and utilization, child health, and parental health during the COVID-19 pandemic. Methods The sample includes children from households with incomes up to 138% of the Federal Poverty Level using data from the 2016-2023 National Survey of Children's Health. Child outcomes include insurance coverage, usual place for sick care, personal doctor or nurse, unmet healthcare needs, past 12-month utilization of any medical services, preventive medical and dental visits, mental health professional treatments, non-mental health specialist visits, emergency department visits, hospitalizations, and general/oral health ratings. Parent outcomes include physical and mental health ratings. Difference-in-differences models estimate year-by-year differences in outcomes between Medicaid expansion and non-expansion states. Results There is a decline in usual place for sick care, personal doctor or nurse, and preventive medical visits in the first or second pandemic year and in child health in 2020/2023 in expansion versus non-expansion states. In contrast, there is an increase in mental health professional treatments and other specialist visits in 2021-2023 in expansion states. Lastly, there are declines in maternal physical/mental health and father's mental health in 2020 but increase in father's mental/physical health in 2023 in expansion states. Conclusion During the pandemic, children in low-income households had less care continuity and preventive care but more mental health treatments and non-mental specialist visits in Medicaid expansion versus non-expansion states. There were also declines in both child and parent health. Findings potentially reflect intertwined effects from greater parental coverage but more pandemic-related social mobility restrictions disrupting routine/preventive care and worsening mental health more in expansion states.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate whether pre-pandemic state Medicaid expansions for adults influenced children's healthcare access and utilization, child health, and parental health during the COVID-19 pandemic.
Design and population
The study used data from the 2016-2023 National Survey of Children's Health, focusing on children from households with incomes up to 138% of the Federal Poverty Level.
Core methods
Difference-in-differences models were employed to estimate year-by-year differences in outcomes between Medicaid expansion and non-expansion states.
Main results
There was a decline in usual place for sick care, personal doctor or nurse, and preventive medical visits in the first or second pandemic year and in child health in 2020/2023 in expansion versus non-expansion states. In contrast, there was an increase in mental health professional treatments and other specialist visits in 2021-2023 in expansion states.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Medicaid expansions may have had mixed effects on children's healthcare, with reduced continuity of care and preventive services but increased mental health treatments and specialist visits during the pandemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results highlight the complex impact of Medicaid expansions on children's healthcare access and health during the pandemic, informing future policy decisions and public health strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on observational data and difference-in-differences models, which may be subject to confounding factors and limitations in causal inference.

自然 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染后的核衣壳和刺突特异性同种型体液 免疫应答 与国药集团、科兴和阿斯利康的 疫苗接种 的比较分析。

Comparative Analysis of Nucleocapsid- and Spike-Specific Isotype Humoral Immune Responses Following Natural SARS-CoV-2 Infection and Vaccination with Sinopharm, Sinovac, and AstraZeneca.
作者： Mohamed Hassan Nasraa, Dina Nadeem Abd-Elshafy, Rola Nadeem, Said I. Shalaby, Khaled Amer, Wael Ali Hassan, Ahmed Hamdy Bader, Hasnaa A. ElShehaby, Mahmoud Mohamed Bahgat, Dina Nadeem Abd‐Elshafy
摘要中文翻译严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒 2 (严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2) 疫苗 平台的多样性和广泛的自然暴露创造了复杂的体液免疫景观,需要详细表征。在这里,我们使用商业 ELISA 试剂盒比较了第一波大流行期间(2020 年 4 月至 8 月)感染者 (IH) 和 疫苗 受者(国药控股、科兴或阿斯利康)血清中核衣壳 (N) 和刺突 (S) 特异性抗体反应。在自然 IH 中,N 的 IgG 阳性率 (80.9%) 高于 S (42.8%),与流行前对照相比,N 特异性 IgG 水平显着升高 (p 值 <0.05),而 IgM 反应没有显示显着差异。在 S 和 N 特异性抗体反应之间观察到强烈的正相关性,特别是对于 IgG 及其亚类。接种疫苗的 队列研究 对两种低 IgM 水平的抗原表现出高 IgG 阳性率 (83-100%),总体 IgG 和亚类反应相对于大流行前对照显着升高 (p 值 <0.05),各组之间的抗原间相关性始终很强。总体而言,自然感染会诱导偏向核衣壳抗原的协调体液反应,支持其作为近期感染标志物的实用性,而疫苗接种则引发强大的多亚类抗体反应,在抗原识别和亚类分布方面具有平台依赖性差异。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在表征自然感染后的体液 免疫应答 至 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 核衣壳 (N) 和刺突 (S) 抗原以及不同平台的 疫苗接种,因为 疫苗 平台和自然暴露的多样性创造了复杂的免疫景观,需要详细了解。
设计与对象
该研究比较了第一波大流行期间(2020 年 4 月至 8 月)自然 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染者的血清中的 N 和 S 特异性抗体反应,以及接种了国药集团、科兴或阿斯利康疫苗的 疫苗 接受者的血清中的 N 和 S 特异性抗体反应。
核心方法
使用商业 ELISA 试剂盒测量抗体反应,以评估感染者和 疫苗 受体血清中 N 和 S 抗原的 IgG 和 IgM 阳性率。
主要结果
在自然感染个体中,N 的 IgG 阳性率 (80.9%) 高于 S (42.8%),与大流行前对照相比,N 特异性 IgG 水平显着升高(p 值 <0.05)。接种疫苗的 队列研究 对两种低 IgM 水平的抗原表现出高 IgG 阳性率 (83-100%),并且总体 IgG 和亚类反应相对于大流行前对照显着升高(p 值 <0.05)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,自然感染会诱导偏向核衣壳抗原的协调体液反应,而 疫苗接种 则会引发强大的多亚类抗体反应,在抗原识别和亚类分布方面具有平台依赖性差异。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果支持使用核衣壳特异性抗体作为最近 严重急性呼吸综合征冠状病毒2 感染的标记,并强调了解 疫苗 诱导的 免疫应答 中平台依赖性差异对于改进 监测 和 疫苗 发育的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于第一波大流行的血清,并未考虑长期免疫或变异特异性反应,但由于使用经过验证的 ELISA 方法直接比较抗体反应,因此证据充足。

Comparative Analysis of Nucleocapsid- and Spike-Specific Isotype Humoral Immune Responses Following Natural SARS-CoV-2 Infection and Vaccination with Sinopharm, Sinovac, and AstraZeneca.

Authors: Mohamed Hassan Nasraa, Dina Nadeem Abd-Elshafy, Rola Nadeem, Said I. Shalaby, Khaled Amer, Wael Ali Hassan, Ahmed Hamdy Bader, Hasnaa A. ElShehaby, Mahmoud Mohamed Bahgat, Dina Nadeem Abd‐Elshafy
Original abstract (English)
The diversity of severe acute respiratory syndrome coronavirus 2 (SARS-CoV-2) vaccine platforms and widespread natural exposure have created a complex landscape of humoral immunity requiring detailed characterization. Here, we compare nucleocapsid (N)- and spike (S)-specific antibody responses in sera from infected humans (IH) during the first pandemic wave (April–August 2020) and in vaccine recipients (Sinopharm, Sinovac, or AstraZeneca) using commercial ELISA kits. Among naturally IH, the IgG-positivity rate was higher for N (80.9%) than S (42.8%), with significantly elevated N-specific IgG levels compared to prepandemic controls ( p -value <0.05), while IgM responses showed no significant differences. Strong positive correlations were observed between S- and N-specific antibody responses, particularly for IgG and its subclasses. Vaccinated cohorts exhibited high IgG positivity rate (83–100%) against both antigens with low IgM levels, and overall IgG and subclass responses were significantly elevated relative to pre-pandemic controls ( p -value <0.05), with consistently strong inter-antigen correlations across groups. Overall, natural infection induces a coordinated humoral response biased toward the nucleocapsid antigen, supporting its utility as a marker of recent infection, whereas vaccination elicits a robust multi-subclass antibody response with platform-dependent differences in antigen recognition and subclass distribution.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to characterize the humoral immune responses to SARS-CoV-2 nucleocapsid (N) and spike (S) antigens following natural infection and vaccination with different platforms, as the diversity of vaccine platforms and natural exposure has created a complex landscape of immunity requiring detailed understanding.
Design and population
The study compared N- and S-specific antibody responses in sera from individuals with natural SARS-CoV-2 infection during the first pandemic wave (April–August 2020) and in vaccine recipients who had been vaccinated with Sinopharm, Sinovac, or AstraZeneca.
Core methods
Antibody responses were measured using commercial ELISA kits to assess IgG and IgM positivity rates against N and S antigens in sera from infected humans and vaccine recipients.
Main results
Among naturally infected individuals, the IgG-positivity rate was higher for N (80.9%) than S (42.8%), with significantly elevated N-specific IgG levels compared to prepandemic controls (p-value <0.05). Vaccinated cohorts showed high IgG positivity rates (83–100%) against both antigens with low IgM levels, and overall IgG and subclass responses were significantly elevated relative to pre-pandemic controls (p-value <0.05).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that natural infection induces a coordinated humoral response biased toward the nucleocapsid antigen, whereas vaccination elicits a robust multi-subclass antibody response with platform-dependent differences in antigen recognition and subclass distribution.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results support the use of nucleocapsid-specific antibodies as a marker for recent SARS-CoV-2 infection and highlight the importance of understanding platform-dependent differences in vaccine-induced immune responses for improved surveillance and vaccine development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to sera from the first pandemic wave and does not account for long-term immunity or variant-specific responses, but the evidence is strong due to the direct comparison of antibody responses using validated ELISA methods.
